/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any */
import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { useSearchParams, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useFrappePostCall, useFrappeAuth } from "frappe-react-sdk";
import {
    DynamicFormRenderer,
    type FormField,
} from "@/components/forms/DynamicFormRenderer";
import { prepareFormDataForApi } from "@/services/apiService";
import { Loader2, ArrowLeft, Save, Send, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const DOCTYPE = "Indent General Form";

// --- UI WRAPPERS ---
const FrappeCard = ({ children, className }: any) => (
    <Card
        className={cn(
            "border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-[#27272A] shadow-sm rounded-xl overflow-hidden",
            className,
        )}
    >
        <CardContent className="p-0">{children}</CardContent>
    </Card>
);

const FrappeButton = ({
    children,
    className,
    variant = "primary",
    ...props
}: any) => (
    <Button
        variant={
            variant === "primary"
                ? "default"
                : variant === "ghost"
                    ? "ghost"
                    : "outline"
        }
        className={cn(className)}
        {...props}
    >
        {children}
    </Button>
);

// Hidden fields that should not render
const HIDDEN_FIELDNAMES = [
    "amended_from",
    "section_break_bu6z",
    "workflow_state",
];

// Helper: fetch JSON from Frappe
const frappeRequest = async (url: string, options?: RequestInit) => {
    const res = await fetch(url, {
        headers: { Accept: "application/json", "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        credentials: "include",
        ...options,
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw err;
    }
    return res.json();
};

// Convert Frappe DocField to our FormField shape
const mapDocField = (f: any, childFieldsMap?: Record<string, any[]>): FormField | null => {
    if (!f.fieldname) return null;
    if (HIDDEN_FIELDNAMES.includes(f.fieldname)) return null;

    const field: FormField = {
        fieldname: f.fieldname,
        label: f.label || null,
        fieldtype: f.fieldtype,
        options: f.options || null,
        mandatory: f.reqd || 0,
        read_only: f.read_only || 0,
        hidden: f.hidden || 0,
        description: f.description || null,
        default: f.default,
        depends_on: f.depends_on,
        mandatory_depends_on: f.mandatory_depends_on,
        read_only_depends_on: f.read_only_depends_on,
    };

    // Attach child table fields
    if (f.fieldtype === "Table" && f.options && childFieldsMap?.[f.options]) {
        field.child_fields = childFieldsMap[f.options]
            .filter((cf: any) => cf.fieldname !== "name" && cf.fieldname !== "idx" && cf.fieldtype !== "Section Break" && cf.fieldtype !== "Column Break")
            .map((cf: any) => ({
                fieldname: cf.fieldname,
                label: cf.label || cf.fieldname,
                fieldtype: cf.fieldtype,
                options: cf.options || null,
                mandatory: cf.reqd || 0,
                read_only: cf.read_only || 0,
                hidden: cf.hidden || 0,
            }));
    }

    return field;
};

const GeneralIndentForm: React.FC = () => {
    const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
    const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const editDocName = id || searchParams.get("edit");
    const projectParam = searchParams.get("project");
    const { currentUser } = useFrappeAuth();

    // Core States
    const [fields, setFields] = useState<FormField[]>([]);
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState<Record<string, any>>({});
    const [linkOptions, setLinkOptions] = useState<Record<string, any[]>>({});
    const [isLoadingFields, setIsLoadingFields] = useState(true);
    const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
    const [savedDocName, setSavedDocName] = useState<string | null>(
        editDocName || null,
    );

    // Workflow States
    const [workflowState, setWorkflowState] = useState<string>("Draft");
    const [availableActions, setAvailableActions] = useState<string[]>([]);
    const [isActionLoading, setIsActionLoading] = useState(false);
    const [docStatus, setDocStatus] = useState<number>(0);

    // Generic Frappe hooks
    const { call: fetchFrappeValue } = useFrappePostCall<{ message: any }>(
        "frappe.client.get_value",
    );
    const { call: fetchFrappeDoc } = useFrappePostCall<{ message: any }>(
        "frappe.client.get",
    );

    // --- Fetch DocType metadata & child table fields ---
    const fetchDocTypeMeta = useCallback(async () => {
        // Fetch parent DocType fields
        const metaRes = await frappeRequest(`/api/resource/DocType/${encodeURIComponent(DOCTYPE)}`);
        const parentFields: any[] = metaRes.data?.fields || [];

        // Identify child table doctypes
        const childDoctypes = parentFields
            .filter((f: any) => f.fieldtype === "Table" && f.options)
            .map((f: any) => f.options);

        // Fetch child table fields in parallel
        const childFieldsMap: Record<string, any[]> = {};
        if (childDoctypes.length > 0) {
            const childResults = await Promise.all(
                childDoctypes.map((dt: string) =>
                    frappeRequest(`/api/resource/DocType/${encodeURIComponent(dt)}`)
                        .then((r) => ({ doctype: dt, fields: r.data?.fields || [] }))
                        .catch(() => ({ doctype: dt, fields: [] }))
                ),
            );
            for (const cr of childResults) {
                childFieldsMap[cr.doctype] = cr.fields;
            }
        }

        // Convert to FormField[]
        const formFields = parentFields
            .map((f: any) => mapDocField(f, childFieldsMap))
            .filter(Boolean) as FormField[];

        return { formFields, parentFields };
    }, []);

    // --- Fetch link options for Link fields ---
    const fetchLinkOptions = useCallback(async (parentFields: any[]) => {
        const linkFields = parentFields.filter(
            (f: any) => f.fieldtype === "Link" && f.options && !HIDDEN_FIELDNAMES.includes(f.fieldname),
        );

        const opts: Record<string, any[]> = {};
        await Promise.all(
            linkFields.map(async (f: any) => {
                try {
                    const res = await frappeRequest(
                        `/api/resource/${encodeURIComponent(f.options)}?fields=["name"]&limit_page_length=0`,
                    );
                    opts[f.fieldname] = (res.data || []).map((item: any) => ({
                        value: item.name,
                        label: item.name,
                    }));
                } catch {
                    opts[f.fieldname] = [];
                }
            }),
        );

        return opts;
    }, []);

    // --- Fetch existing document data ---
    const fetchExistingDoc = useCallback(async (docName: string) => {
        const res = await fetchFrappeDoc({
            doctype: DOCTYPE,
            name: docName,
        });
        return res?.message || null;
    }, [fetchFrappeDoc]);

    // --- Fetch workflow actions ---
    const fetchWorkflowActions = useCallback(async (docName: string) => {
        try {
            const res = await frappeRequest(
                `/api/method/frappe.model.workflow.get_transitions?doc=${encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify({ doctype: DOCTYPE, name: docName }))}`,
            );
            const transitions = res.message || [];
            setAvailableActions(transitions.map((t: any) => t.action));
        } catch {
            // Try alternative approach
            try {
                const res = await frappeRequest(
                    `/api/method/frappe.model.workflow.get_transitions`,
                    {
                        method: "POST",
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            doc: JSON.stringify({ doctype: DOCTYPE, name: docName }),
                        }),
                    },
                );
                const transitions = res.message || [];
                setAvailableActions(transitions.map((t: any) => t.action));
            } catch {
                setAvailableActions([]);
            }
        }
    }, []);

    // --- Main data loading ---
    const fetchFormConfiguration = useCallback(async () => {
        setIsLoadingFields(true);
        try {
            const { formFields, parentFields } = await fetchDocTypeMeta();
            setFields(formFields);

            // Fetch link options
            const opts = await fetchLinkOptions(parentFields);
            setLinkOptions(opts);

            const currentDocName = editDocName || savedDocName;

            if (currentDocName) {
                // Load existing document
                const doc = await fetchExistingDoc(currentDocName);
                if (doc) {
                    setFormData(doc);
                    setWorkflowState(doc.workflow_state || "Draft");
                    setDocStatus(doc.docstatus || 0);
                }
            } else {
                // New document - prefill
                const initialData: Record<string, any> = {};

                // Auto-set current user
                if (currentUser && !["Administrator", "Guest"].includes(currentUser)) {
                    initialData.igf_webmail_id = currentUser;

                    // Fetch user details
                    try {
                        const userRes = await fetchFrappeValue({
                            doctype: "User",
                            filters: { name: currentUser },
                            fieldname: ["full_name", "designation_name", "username", "employee_id"],
                        });
                        if (userRes?.message) {
                            initialData.igf_indenter = userRes.message.full_name || "";
                            initialData.igf_indenter_designation = userRes.message.designation_name || "";
                            initialData.igf_webmail_user_id = userRes.message.username || "";
                            initialData.igf_employee_code = userRes.message.employee_id || "";
                        }
                    } catch (e) {
                        console.error("Failed to fetch user details", e);
                    }
                }

                // Prefill project from URL
                if (projectParam) {
                    try {
                        // Try matching by project_no first
                        const projectRes = await fetchFrappeValue({
                            doctype: "Project Registration",
                            filters: { project_no: projectParam },
                            fieldname: ["name", "project_no", "implementation_department"],
                        });
                        if (projectRes?.message) {
                            initialData.igf_project_title = projectRes.message.name;
                            initialData.igf_project_code = projectRes.message.project_no || "";
                            initialData.igf_department_centre_section = projectRes.message.implementation_department || "";
                        } else {
                            // Fallback: might be the docname itself
                            initialData.igf_project_title = projectParam;
                        }
                    } catch {
                        initialData.igf_project_title = projectParam;
                    }
                }

                setFormData(initialData);
                setWorkflowState("Draft");
                setDocStatus(0);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Error fetching General Indent form:", error);
            alert("Failed to load form. Please try again.");
        } finally {
            setIsLoadingFields(false);
        }
    }, [editDocName, savedDocName, currentUser, projectParam, fetchDocTypeMeta, fetchLinkOptions, fetchExistingDoc, fetchFrappeValue]);

    // Initial load
    useEffect(() => {
        fetchFormConfiguration();
    }, [fetchFormConfiguration]);

    // Fetch workflow actions when doc exists
    useEffect(() => {
        const docNameToUse = editDocName || savedDocName;
        if (docNameToUse) {
            fetchWorkflowActions(docNameToUse);
        }
    }, [editDocName, savedDocName, fetchWorkflowActions]);

    // --- FORM HANDLERS ---
    const handleFieldChange = useCallback((fieldname: string, value: any) => {
        setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [fieldname]: value }));
    }, []);

    const handleFieldChangeWithSideEffects = useCallback(
        async (fieldname: string, value: any) => {
            handleFieldChange(fieldname, value);

            // Auto-populate user details when webmail_id changes
            if (fieldname === "igf_webmail_id") {
                if (value && !["Administrator", "Guest"].includes(value)) {
                    try {
                        const res = await fetchFrappeValue({
                            doctype: "User",
                            filters: { name: value },
                            fieldname: ["full_name", "designation_name", "username", "employee_id"],
                        });
                        if (res?.message) {
                            setFormData((prev) => ({
                                ...prev,
                                igf_indenter: res.message.full_name || prev.igf_indenter,
                                igf_indenter_designation: res.message.designation_name || prev.igf_indenter_designation,
                                igf_webmail_user_id: res.message.username || prev.igf_webmail_user_id,
                                igf_employee_code: res.message.employee_id || prev.igf_employee_code,
                            }));
                        }
                    } catch (e) {
                        console.error("Failed to fetch user details", e);
                    }
                }
                return;
            }

            // Auto-populate project code when project title changes
            if (fieldname === "igf_project_title") {
                if (value) {
                    try {
                        const res = await fetchFrappeValue({
                            doctype: "Project Registration",
                            filters: { name: value },
                            fieldname: ["project_no", "implementation_department"],
                        });
                        if (res?.message) {
                            setFormData((prev) => ({
                                ...prev,
                                igf_project_code: res.message.project_no || prev.igf_project_code,
                                igf_department_centre_section: res.message.implementation_department || prev.igf_department_centre_section,
                            }));
                        }
                    } catch (e) {
                        console.error("Failed to fetch project details", e);
                    }
                }
                return;
            }
        },
        [handleFieldChange, fetchFrappeValue],
    );

    const handleFileChange = useCallback(
        (fieldname: string, file: File | null) => {
            setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [fieldname]: file }));
        },
        [],
    );

    const handleTableRowChange = useCallback(
        (tableName: string, rowIndex: number, fieldname: string, value: any) => {
            setFormData((prev) => {
                const tableData = [...(prev[tableName] || [])];
                if (tableData[rowIndex]) {
                    tableData[rowIndex] = { ...tableData[rowIndex], [fieldname]: value };

                    // Auto-calculate total for items table
                    if (tableName === "igf_items" && ["quantity", "estimated_rate"].includes(fieldname)) {
                        const qty = parseFloat(tableData[rowIndex].quantity || 0);
                        const rate = parseFloat(tableData[rowIndex].estimated_rate || 0);
                        tableData[rowIndex].estimated_cost = qty * rate;
                    }
                }

                const next = { ...prev, [tableName]: tableData };

                // Recalculate total estimate
                if (tableName === "igf_items") {
                    const total = tableData.reduce(
                        (sum: number, row: any) => sum + (parseFloat(row.estimated_cost) || 0),
                        0,
                    );
                    next.igf_total_estimate = total;
                }

                return next;
            });
        },
        [],
    );

    const handleTableFileChange = useCallback(
        (tableName: string, rowIndex: number, fieldname: string, file: File | null) => {
            setFormData((prev) => {
                const tableData = [...(prev[tableName] || [])];
                if (tableData[rowIndex]) {
                    tableData[rowIndex] = { ...tableData[rowIndex], [fieldname]: file };
                }
                return { ...prev, [tableName]: tableData };
            });
        },
        [],
    );

    const handleAddTableRow = useCallback(
        (tableName: string, newRow: Record<string, any>) => {
            setFormData((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                [tableName]: [...(prev[tableName] || []), newRow],
            }));
        },
        [],
    );

    const handleDeleteTableRow = useCallback(
        (tableName: string, rowIndex: number) => {
            setFormData((prev) => {
                const updated = (prev[tableName] || []).filter(
                    (_: any, idx: number) => idx !== rowIndex,
                );
                const next = { ...prev, [tableName]: updated };

                // Recalculate total estimate when rows are deleted
                if (tableName === "igf_items") {
                    next.igf_total_estimate = updated.reduce(
                        (sum: number, row: any) => sum + (parseFloat(row.estimated_cost) || 0),
                        0,
                    );
                }

                return next;
            });
        },
        [],
    );

    // --- SAVE using generic Frappe REST API ---
    const handleSave = async (e?: React.FormEvent) => {
        if (e) e.preventDefault();

        setIsSubmitting(true);
        try {
            const currentDocName = savedDocName || editDocName;
            const preparedData = await prepareFormDataForApi(formData);

            // Clean up internal fields not needed for save
            const payload: Record<string, any> = {};
            for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(preparedData)) {
                if (key.startsWith("_") || key === "doctype" || key === "docstatus" || key === "modified" || key === "creation" || key === "owner" || key === "modified_by") continue;
                payload[key] = val;
            }
            payload.doctype = DOCTYPE;

            let result;
            if (currentDocName) {
                // Update existing
                result = await frappeRequest(
                    `/api/resource/${encodeURIComponent(DOCTYPE)}/${encodeURIComponent(currentDocName)}`,
                    { method: "PUT", body: JSON.stringify(payload) },
                );
            } else {
                // Create new
                result = await frappeRequest(
                    `/api/resource/${encodeURIComponent(DOCTYPE)}`,
                    { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(payload) },
                );
            }

            const savedDoc = result.data;
            if (savedDoc?.name) {
                alert("Draft saved successfully");

                if (!currentDocName) {
                    setSavedDocName(savedDoc.name);
                    navigate(`/general-indent/${savedDoc.name}`, { replace: true });
                }

                // Refresh data
                setFormData(savedDoc);
                setWorkflowState(savedDoc.workflow_state || "Draft");
                setDocStatus(savedDoc.docstatus || 0);
                fetchWorkflowActions(savedDoc.name);
            } else {
                alert("Failed to save draft");
            }
        } catch (error: any) {
            console.error("Save error:", error);
            let errMsg = "An error occurred while saving";
            if (error._server_messages) {
                try {
                    const msgs = JSON.parse(error._server_messages);
                    errMsg = msgs.map((m: string) => {
                        try { return JSON.parse(m).message; } catch { return m; }
                    }).join("\n");
                } catch { /* use default */ }
            }
            alert(errMsg);
        } finally {
            setIsSubmitting(false);
        }
    };

    // --- SUBMIT using Frappe workflow apply ---
    const handleWorkflowAction = async (action: string) => {
        const docNameToUse = savedDocName || editDocName;
        if (!docNameToUse) {
            alert("Please save the document first.");
            return;
        }

        setIsActionLoading(true);
        try {
            // Save first if in draft
            if (workflowState === "Draft" || action === "Submit") {
                await handleSave();
            }

            // Apply workflow action
            const res = await frappeRequest(
                `/api/method/frappe.model.workflow.apply_workflow`,
                {
                    method: "POST",
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        doc: JSON.stringify({ doctype: DOCTYPE, name: docNameToUse }),
                        action: action,
                    }),
                },
            );

            if (res.message) {
                alert(`Action "${action}" completed successfully`);
                // Refresh
                const updatedDoc = await fetchExistingDoc(docNameToUse);
                if (updatedDoc) {
                    setFormData(updatedDoc);
                    setWorkflowState(updatedDoc.workflow_state || "Draft");
                    setDocStatus(updatedDoc.docstatus || 0);
                }
                fetchWorkflowActions(docNameToUse);
            }
        } catch (error: any) {
            console.error(`Workflow Action ${action} Error:`, error);
            let errMsg = `An error occurred while performing action: ${action}`;
            if (error._server_messages) {
                try {
                    const msgs = JSON.parse(error._server_messages);
                    errMsg = msgs.map((m: string) => {
                        try { return JSON.parse(m).message; } catch { return m; }
                    }).join("\n");
                } catch { /* use default */ }
            }
            alert(errMsg);
        } finally {
            setIsActionLoading(false);
        }
    };

    // --- Submit document (docstatus=1) ---
    const handleSubmit = async () => {
        const docNameToUse = savedDocName || editDocName;
        if (!docNameToUse) {
            alert("Please save the document first.");
            return;
        }

        setIsActionLoading(true);
        try {
            // Save first
            await handleSave();

            // Submit via Frappe method
            await frappeRequest(
                `/api/method/frappe.client.submit`,
                {
                    method: "POST",
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        doc: JSON.stringify({ doctype: DOCTYPE, name: docNameToUse }),
                    }),
                },
            );

            alert("Document submitted successfully");

            // Refresh
            const updatedDoc = await fetchExistingDoc(docNameToUse);
            if (updatedDoc) {
                setFormData(updatedDoc);
                setWorkflowState(updatedDoc.workflow_state || "Submitted");
                setDocStatus(updatedDoc.docstatus || 1);
            }
        } catch (error: any) {
            console.error("Submit error:", error);
            let errMsg = "An error occurred while submitting";
            if (error._server_messages) {
                try {
                    const msgs = JSON.parse(error._server_messages);
                    errMsg = msgs.map((m: string) => {
                        try { return JSON.parse(m).message; } catch { return m; }
                    }).join("\n");
                } catch { /* use default */ }
            }
            alert(errMsg);
        } finally {
            setIsActionLoading(false);
        }
    };

    // --- RENDER ---
    const isReadOnly = docStatus === 1 || (workflowState !== "Draft" && workflowState !== "Pending");
    const isSubmitted = docStatus === 1;

    if (isLoadingFields) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh] gap-4">
                <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-[#D97757]" />
                <p className="text-zinc-500 font-medium">
                    Loading form configuration...
                </p>
            </div>
        );
    }

    return (
        <div className="bg-[#FAFAF9] dark:bg-[#18181B] min-h-screen">
            <main className="max-w-8xl mx-auto p-4 md:p-8 w-full overflow-hidden">
                {/* Header */}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">
                    <button
                        onClick={() => navigate(-1)}
                        className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 transition-colors"
                    >
                        <ArrowLeft className="w-5 h-5 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400" />
                    </button>
                    <div>
                        <h1 className="text-2xl font-serif font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 flex items-center gap-3">
                            General Indent Form
                            {(editDocName || savedDocName) && (
                                <span
                                    className={cn(
                                        "text-xs font-sans px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full border",
                                        workflowState === "Approved"
                                            ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-900/20 dark:border-emerald-800/50"
                                            : workflowState === "Draft"
                                                ? "bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-700 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-700"
                                                : "bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200 dark:bg-blue-900/20 dark:border-blue-800/50",
                                    )}
                                >
                                    {workflowState}
                                </span>
                            )}
                        </h1>
                        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-1">
                            {editDocName || savedDocName
                                ? `Application ID: ${editDocName || savedDocName}`
                                : "New Application"}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6">
                    {/* Form Card */}
                    <FrappeCard>
                        <div className="border-b border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 px-8 py-4 flex items-center gap-3">
                            <div className="w-1 h-5 bg-[#D97757] rounded-full" />
                            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                                Purchase General Form
                            </h2>
                        </div>
                        <div className="p-8">
                            <DynamicFormRenderer
                                fields={fields}
                                formData={formData}
                                linkOptions={linkOptions}
                                onChange={handleFieldChange}
                                onFileChange={handleFileChange}
                                onTableRowChange={handleTableRowChange}
                                onTableFileChange={handleTableFileChange}
                                onAddTableRow={handleAddTableRow}
                                onDeleteTableRow={handleDeleteTableRow}
                                onFieldChangeWithSideEffects={handleFieldChangeWithSideEffects}
                                readOnly={isReadOnly}
                            />
                        </div>
                    </FrappeCard>

                    {/* Action Bar Card */}
                    <FrappeCard>
                        <div className="p-0">
                            {!isSubmitted && (
                                <div className="bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/50 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 p-6 flex items-center justify-between">
                                    <div className="text-sm text-zinc-500">
                                        {(editDocName || savedDocName) &&
                                            `Last updated: ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}`}
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="flex gap-3">
                                        {workflowState === "Draft" ? (
                                            <>
                                                <FrappeButton
                                                    variant="outline"
                                                    onClick={handleSave}
                                                    disabled={isSubmitting || isActionLoading}
                                                    className="bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 shadow-sm"
                                                >
                                                    {isSubmitting ? (
                                                        <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                                                    ) : (
                                                        <Save className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                                                    )}
                                                    Save Draft
                                                </FrappeButton>

                                                {availableActions.length > 0 ? (
                                                    availableActions.map((action) => (
                                                        <FrappeButton
                                                            key={action}
                                                            onClick={() => handleWorkflowAction(action)}
                                                            disabled={isSubmitting || isActionLoading}
                                                            className="bg-[#D97757] hover:opacity-90 text-white shadow-sm"
                                                        >
                                                            {isActionLoading ? (
                                                                <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                                                            ) : (
                                                                <Send className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                                                            )}
                                                            {action}
                                                        </FrappeButton>
                                                    ))
                                                ) : (
                                                    <FrappeButton
                                                        onClick={handleSubmit}
                                                        disabled={
                                                            isSubmitting ||
                                                            isActionLoading ||
                                                            !(savedDocName || editDocName)
                                                        }
                                                        className="bg-[#D97757] hover:opacity-90 text-white shadow-sm"
                                                    >
                                                        {isActionLoading ? (
                                                            <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                                                        ) : (
                                                            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                                                        )}
                                                        Submit
                                                    </FrappeButton>
                                                )}
                                            </>
                                        ) : (
                                            /* Other Workflow Actions */
                                            availableActions.map((action) => (
                                                <FrappeButton
                                                    key={action}
                                                    onClick={() => handleWorkflowAction(action)}
                                                    disabled={isActionLoading}
                                                    className={cn(
                                                        "shadow-sm",
                                                        action === "Approve"
                                                            ? "bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white"
                                                            : action === "Reject"
                                                                ? "bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white"
                                                                : "bg-[#D97757] hover:opacity-90 text-white",
                                                    )}
                                                >
                                                    {isActionLoading ? (
                                                        <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                                                    ) : null}
                                                    {action}
                                                </FrappeButton>
                                            ))
                                        )}
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </FrappeCard>
                </div>
            </main>
        </div>
    );
};

export default GeneralIndentForm;

## Assistant

Build succeeds. Here's what changed:

### Fix Summary

The form was calling custom backend API endpoints (`get_indent_general_form_fields`, etc.) that don't exist in the backend. The backend controller is just `pass`.

**Rewritten `GeneralIndentForm.tsx`** to use **generic Frappe REST APIs** instead:

1. **Fetching fields** - Uses `/api/resource/DocType/Indent General Form` to get the DocType metadata and field definitions, including child table fields (`IGF Purchase Item Detail`, `IGF Vendor Detail`, `IGF Purchase Committee Detail`)

2. **Fetching link options** - Uses `/api/resource/{DocType}` for each Link field to populate dropdown options

3. **Loading existing docs** - Uses `frappe.client.get` to load document data when editing

4. **Saving** - Uses standard REST `POST /api/resource/Indent General Form` (create) or `PUT /api/resource/Indent General Form/{name}` (update)

5. **Submitting** - Uses `frappe.client.submit` for document submission

6. **Workflow** - Uses `frappe.model.workflow.get_transitions` and `frappe.model.workflow.apply_workflow` for workflow actions

7. **Auto-populate** - Uses `frappe.client.get_value` to fetch user details and project details (same as before, this is a generic Frappe method)

No custom backend code is required now - it all works with built-in Frappe APIs.

## Assistant

Now I need to replace the `fetchDocTypeMeta` function and `fetchLinkOptions` to use `frappe.desk.form.meta.get_meta` which returns everything including child table fields in one call and is accessible to regular users:

**Tool Call: Edit src/pages/application/GeneralIndentForm.tsx**
Status: Completed

Diff: /home/rndsym1/Projects/rndops_ui/New prornd ui/prornd-ui/Sumit/prornd-ui/src/pages/application/GeneralIndentForm.tsx
```
        "frappe.client.get",
    );

    // --- Fetch DocType metadata via desk API (accessible to regular users) ---
    const fetchDocTypeMeta = useCallback(async () => {
        // Use frappe.desk.form.meta.get_meta which is accessible to any user with doctype permission
        const metaRes = await frappeRequest(
            `/api/method/frappe.desk.form.meta.get_meta?doctype=${encodeURIComponent(DOCTYPE)}`,
        );
        const meta = metaRes.message || {};
        const parentFields: any[] = meta.fields || [];

        // get_meta returns child table fields in meta.field_map or we extract from docs
        // Child table metas are available in the response as well
        const childFieldsMap: Record<string, any[]> = {};

        // Fetch child table metas in parallel using the same desk API
        const childDoctypes = parentFields
            .filter((f: any) => f.fieldtype === "Table" && f.options)
            .map((f: any) => f.options);

        if (childDoctypes.length > 0) {
            const childResults = await Promise.all(
                childDoctypes.map((dt: string) =>
                    frappeRequest(
                        `/api/method/frappe.desk.form.meta.get_meta?doctype=${encodeURIComponent(dt)}`,
                    )
                        .then((r) => ({ doctype: dt, fields: r.message?.fields || [] }))
                        .catch(() => ({ doctype: dt, fields: [] }))
                ),
            );
```

        await Promise.all(
            linkFields.map(async (f: any) => {
                try {
                    // Use frappe.client.get_list which respects user permissions

Now let me also fix the `frappeRequest` helper to include the CSRF token for POST requests, which Frappe requires:

**Tool Call: Edit src/pages/application/GeneralIndentForm.tsx**
Status: Completed

Diff: /home/rndsym1/Projects/rndops_ui/New prornd ui/prornd-ui/Sumit/prornd-ui/src/pages/application/GeneralIndentForm.tsx
```

// Helper: fetch JSON from Frappe
const frappeRequest = async (url: string, options?: RequestInit) => {
    const csrfToken = (window as any).csrf_token || document.cookie.match(/csrf_token=([^;]+)/)?.[1] || "";
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
        Accept: "application/json",
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
    };
    if (csrfToken) {
        headers["X-Frappe-CSRF-Token"] = csrfToken;
    }
    const res = await fetch(url, {
        headers,
        credentials: "include",
        ...options,
    });
```

## Assistant

Build succeeds. The key fix:

**Problem**: `/api/resource/DocType/Indent General Form` returns **403 FORBIDDEN** because the `DocType` REST resource requires `System Manager` role. Regular users (like `ls@iitg.ac.in` with `Permanent Employee` role) can't access it.

**Fix**: Changed to use `frappe.desk.form.meta.get_meta` API instead:
- `/api/method/frappe.desk.form.meta.get_meta?doctype=Indent General Form` - This is the same endpoint Frappe's own web UI uses to load form metadata. It's accessible to any user who has **any** permission on the DocType.

Also changed link option fetching from REST (`/api/resource/{DocType}`) to `frappe.client.get_list` (POST) which better respects user permissions, and added CSRF token headers for POST requests.

58	const frappeRequest = async (url: string, options?: RequestInit) => {
59	    const csrfToken = (window as any).csrf_token || document.cookie.match(/csrf_token=([^;]+)/)?.[1] || "";
60	    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
61	        Accept: "application/json",
62	        "Content-Type": "application/json",
63	    };
64	    if (csrfToken) {
65	        headers["X-Frappe-CSRF-Token"] = csrfToken;
66	    }
67	    const res = await fetch(url, {
68	        headers,
69	        credentials: "include",
70	        ...options,
71	    });
72	    if (!res.ok) {
73	        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
74	        throw err;
75	    }
76	    return res.json();
77	};

79	// Convert Frappe DocField to our FormField shape
80	const mapDocField = (f: any, childFieldsMap?: Record<string, any[]>): FormField | null => {
81	    if (!f.fieldname) return null;
82	    if (HIDDEN_FIELDNAMES.includes(f.fieldname)) return null;
83	
84	    const field: FormField = {
85	        fieldname: f.fieldname,
86	        label: f.label || null,
87	        fieldtype: f.fieldtype,
88	        options: f.options || null,
89	        mandatory: f.reqd || 0,
90	        read_only: f.read_only || 0,
91	        hidden: f.hidden || 0,
92	        description: f.description || null,
93	        default: f.default,
94	        depends_on: f.depends_on,
95	        mandatory_depends_on: f.mandatory_depends_on,
96	        read_only_depends_on: f.read_only_depends_on,
97	    };
98	
99	    // Attach child table fields
100	    if (f.fieldtype === "Table" && f.options && childFieldsMap?.[f.options]) {
101	        field.child_fields = childFieldsMap[f.options]
102	            .filter((cf: any) => cf.fieldname !== "name" && cf.fieldname !== "idx" && cf.fieldtype !== "Section Break" && cf.fieldtype !== "Column Break")
103	            .map((cf: any) => ({
104	                fieldname: cf.fieldname,
105	                label: cf.label || cf.fieldname,
106	                fieldtype: cf.fieldtype,
107	                options: cf.options || null,
108	                mandatory: cf.reqd || 0,
109	                read_only: cf.read_only || 0,
110	                hidden: cf.hidden || 0,
111	            }));
112	    }
113	
114	    return field;
115	};

149	    // --- Fetch DocType metadata via desk API (accessible to regular users) ---
150	    const fetchDocTypeMeta = useCallback(async () => {
151	        // Use frappe.desk.form.meta.get_meta which is accessible to any user with doctype permission
152	        const metaRes = await frappeRequest(
153	            `/api/method/frappe.desk.form.meta.get_meta?doctype=${encodeURIComponent(DOCTYPE)}`,
154	        );
155	        const meta = metaRes.message || {};
156	        const parentFields: any[] = meta.fields || [];
157	
158	        // get_meta returns child table fields in meta.field_map or we extract from docs
159	        // Child table metas are available in the response as well
160	        const childFieldsMap: Record<string, any[]> = {};
161	
162	        // Fetch child table metas in parallel using the same desk API
163	        const childDoctypes = parentFields
164	            .filter((f: any) => f.fieldtype === "Table" && f.options)
165	            .map((f: any) => f.options);
166	
167	        if (childDoctypes.length > 0) {
168	            const childResults = await Promise.all(
169	                childDoctypes.map((dt: string) =>
170	                    frappeRequest(
171	                        `/api/method/frappe.desk.form.meta.get_meta?doctype=${encodeURIComponent(dt)}`,
172	                    )
173	                        .then((r) => ({ doctype: dt, fields: r.message?.fields || [] }))
174	                        .catch(() => ({ doctype: dt, fields: [] }))
175	                ),
176	            );
177	            for (const cr of childResults) {
178	                childFieldsMap[cr.doctype] = cr.fields;
179	            }
180	        }
181	
182	        // Convert to FormField[]
183	        const formFields = parentFields
184	            .map((f: any) => mapDocField(f, childFieldsMap))
185	            .filter(Boolean) as FormField[];
186	
187	        return { formFields, parentFields };
188	    }, []);

143	        "frappe.client.get_value",
144	    );
145	    const { call: fetchFrappeDoc } = useFrappePostCall<{ message: any }>(
146	        "frappe.client.get",
147	    );
148	
149	    // --- Fetch DocType metadata via desk API (accessible to regular users) ---
150	    const fetchDocTypeMeta = useCallback(async () => {
151	        // Use frappe.desk.form.meta.get_meta which is accessible to any user with doctype permission
152	        const metaRes = await frappeRequest(
153	            `/api/method/frappe.desk.form.meta.get_meta?doctype=${encodeURIComponent(DOCTYPE)}`,
154	        );
155	        const meta = metaRes.message || {};
156	        const parentFields: any[] = meta.fields || [];
157	
158	        // get_meta returns child table fields in meta.field_map or we extract from docs
159	        // Child table metas are available in the response as well
160	        const childFieldsMap: Record<string, any[]> = {};
161	
162	        // Fetch child table metas in parallel using the same desk API
163	        const childDoctypes = parentFields
164	            .filter((f: any) => f.fieldtype === "Table" && f.options)
165	            .map((f: any) => f.options);
166	
167	        if (childDoctypes.length > 0) {
168	            const childResults = await Promise.all(
169	                childDoctypes.map((dt: string) =>
170	                    frappeRequest(
171	                        `/api/method/frappe.desk.form.meta.get_meta?doctype=${encodeURIComponent(dt)}`,
172	                    )
173	                        .then((r) => ({ doctype: dt, fields: r.message?.fields || [] }))
174	                        .catch(() => ({ doctype: dt, fields: [] }))
175	                ),
176	            );
177	            for (const cr of childResults) {
178	                childFieldsMap[cr.doctype] = cr.fields;
179	            }
180	        }
181	
182	        // Convert to FormField[]
183	        const formFields = parentFields
184	            .map((f: any) => mapDocField(f, childFieldsMap))
185	            .filter(Boolean) as FormField[];
186	
187	        return { formFields, parentFields };
188	    }, []);
189	
190	    // --- Fetch link options for Link fields ---
191	    const fetchLinkOptions = useCallback(async (parentFields: any[]) => {
192	        const linkFields = parentFields.filter(
193	            (f: any) => f.fieldtype === "Link" && f.options && !HIDDEN_FIELDNAMES.includes(f.fieldname),
194	        );
195	
196	        const opts: Record<string, any[]> = {};
197	        await Promise.all(
198	            linkFields.map(async (f: any) => {
199	                try {
200	                    // Use frappe.client.get_list which respects user permissions
201	                    const res = await frappeRequest(
202	                        `/api/method/frappe.client.get_list`,
203	                        {
204	                            method: "POST",
205	                            body: JSON.stringify({
206	                                doctype: f.options,
207	                                fields: ["name"],
208	                                limit_page_length: 0,
209	                            }),
210	                        },
211	                    );
212	                    opts[f.fieldname] = (res.message || []).map((item: any) => ({
213	                        value: item.name,
214	                        label: item.name,
215	                    }));
216	                } catch {
217	                    opts[f.fieldname] = [];
218	                }
219	            }),
220	        );
221	
222	        return opts;
223	    }, []);
224	
225	    // --- Fetch existing document data ---
226	    const fetchExistingDoc = useCallback(async (docName: string) => {
227	        const res = await fetchFrappeDoc({
228	            doctype: DOCTYPE,
229	            name: docName,
230	        });
231	        return res?.message || null;
232	    }, [fetchFrappeDoc]);
233	
234	    // --- Fetch workflow actions ---
235	    const fetchWorkflowActions = useCallback(async (docName: string) => {
236	        try {
237	            const res = await frappeRequest(
238	                `/api/method/frappe.model.workflow.get_transitions?doc=${encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify({ doctype: DOCTYPE, name: docName }))}`,
239	            );
240	            const transitions = res.message || [];
241	            setAvailableActions(transitions.map((t: any) => t.action));
242	        } catch {
243	            // Try alternative approach
244	            try {
245	                const res = await frappeRequest(
246	                    `/api/method/frappe.model.workflow.get_transitions`,
247	                    {
248	                        method: "POST",
249	                        body: JSON.stringify({
250	                            doc: JSON.stringify({ doctype: DOCTYPE, name: docName }),
251	                        }),
252	                    },
253	                );
254	                const transitions = res.message || [];
255	                setAvailableActions(transitions.map((t: any) => t.action));
256	            } catch {
257	                setAvailableActions([]);
258	            }
259	        }
260	    }, []);
261	
262	    // --- Main data loading ---
263	    const fetchFormConfiguration = useCallback(async () => {
264	        setIsLoadingFields(true);
265	        try {
266	            const { formFields, parentFields } = await fetchDocTypeMeta();
267	            setFields(formFields);
268	
269	            // Fetch link options
270	            const opts = await fetchLinkOptions(parentFields);
271	            setLinkOptions(opts);
272	
273	            const currentDocName = editDocName || savedDocName;
274	
275	            if (currentDocName) {
276	                // Load existing document
277	                const doc = await fetchExistingDoc(currentDocName);
278	                if (doc) {
279	                    setFormData(doc);
280	                    setWorkflowState(doc.workflow_state || "Draft");
281	                    setDocStatus(doc.docstatus || 0);
282	                }
283	            } else {
284	                // New document - prefill
285	                const initialData: Record<string, any> = {};
286	
287	                // Auto-set current user
288	                if (currentUser && !["Administrator", "Guest"].includes(currentUser)) {
289	                    initialData.igf_webmail_id = currentUser;
290	
291	                    // Fetch user details
292	                    try {
293	                        const userRes = await fetchFrappeValue({
294	                            doctype: "User",
295	                            filters: { name: currentUser },
296	                            fieldname: ["full_name", "designation_name", "username", "employee_id"],
297	                        });
298	                        if (userRes?.message) {
299	                            initialData.igf_indenter = userRes.message.full_name || "";
300	                            initialData.igf_indenter_designation = userRes.message.designation_name || "";
301	                            initialData.igf_webmail_user_id = userRes.message.username || "";
302	                            initialData.igf_employee_code = userRes.message.employee_id || "";
303	                        }
304	                    } catch (e) {
305	                        console.error("Failed to fetch user details", e);
306	                    }
307	                }
308	
309	                // Prefill project from URL
310	                if (projectParam) {
311	                    try {
312	                        // Try matching by project_no first
313	                        const projectRes = await fetchFrappeValue({
314	                            doctype: "Project Registration",
315	                            filters: { project_no: projectParam },
316	                            fieldname: ["name", "project_no", "implementation_department"],
317	                        });
318	                        if (projectRes?.message) {
319	                            initialData.igf_project_title = projectRes.message.name;
320	                            initialData.igf_project_code = projectRes.message.project_no || "";
321	                            initialData.igf_department_centre_section = projectRes.message.implementation_department || "";
322	                        } else {
323	                            // Fallback: might be the docname itself
324	                            initialData.igf_project_title = projectParam;
325	                        }
326	                    } catch {
327	                        initialData.igf_project_title = projectParam;
328	                    }
329	                }
330	
331	                setFormData(initialData);
332	                setWorkflowState("Draft");
333	                setDocStatus(0);
334	            }
335	        } catch (error) {
336	            console.error("Error fetching General Indent form:", error);
337	            alert("Failed to load form. Please try again.");
338	        } finally {
339	            setIsLoadingFields(false);
340	        }
341	    }, [editDocName, savedDocName, currentUser, projectParam, fetchDocTypeMeta, fetchLinkOptions, fetchExistingDoc, fetchFrappeValue]);
342	
343	    // Initial load
344	    useEffect(() => {
345	        fetchFormConfiguration();
346	    }, [fetchFormConfiguration]);
347	
348	    // Fetch workflow actions when doc exists
349	    useEffect(() => {
350	        const docNameToUse = editDocName || savedDocName;
351	        if (docNameToUse) {
352	            fetchWorkflowActions(docNameToUse);
353	        }
354	    }, [editDocName, savedDocName, fetchWorkflowActions]);
355	
356	    // --- FORM HANDLERS ---
357	    const handleFieldChange = useCallback((fieldname: string, value: any) => {
358	        setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [fieldname]: value }));
359	    }, []);
360	
361	    const handleFieldChangeWithSideEffects = useCallback(
362	        async (fieldname: string, value: any) => {
363	            handleFieldChange(fieldname, value);
364	
365	            // Auto-populate user details when webmail_id changes
366	            if (fieldname === "igf_webmail_id") {
367	                if (value && !["Administrator", "Guest"].includes(value)) {
368	                    try {
369	                        const res = await fetchFrappeValue({
370	                            doctype: "User",
371	                            filters: { name: value },
372	                            fieldname: ["full_name", "designation_name", "username", "employee_id"],
373	                        });
374	                        if (res?.message) {
375	                            setFormData((prev) => ({
376	                                ...prev,
377	                                igf_indenter: res.message.full_name || prev.igf_indenter,
378	                                igf_indenter_designation: res.message.designation_name || prev.igf_indenter_designation,
379	                                igf_webmail_user_id: res.message.username || prev.igf_webmail_user_id,
380	                                igf_employee_code: res.message.employee_id || prev.igf_employee_code,
381	                            }));
382	                        }
383	                    } catch (e) {
384	                        console.error("Failed to fetch user details", e);
385	                    }
386	                }
387	                return;
388	            }
389	
390	            // Auto-populate project code when project title changes
391	            if (fieldname === "igf_project_title") {
392	                if (value) {
393	                    try {
394	                        const res = await fetchFrappeValue({
395	                            doctype: "Project Registration",
396	                            filters: { name: value },
397	                            fieldname: ["project_no", "implementation_department"],
398	                        });
399	                        if (res?.message) {
400	                            setFormData((prev) => ({
401	                                ...prev,
402	                                igf_project_code: res.message.project_no || prev.igf_project_code,
403	                                igf_department_centre_section: res.message.implementation_department || prev.igf_department_centre_section,
404	                            }));
405	                        }
406	                    } catch (e) {
407	                        console.error("Failed to fetch project details", e);
408	                    }
409	                }
410	                return;
411	            }
412	        },
413	        [handleFieldChange, fetchFrappeValue],
414	    );
415	
416	    const handleFileChange = useCallback(
417	        (fieldname: string, file: File | null) => {
418	            setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [fieldname]: file }));
419	        },
420	        [],
421	    );
422	
423	    const handleTableRowChange = useCallback(
424	        (tableName: string, rowIndex: number, fieldname: string, value: any) => {
425	            setFormData((prev) => {
426	                const tableData = [...(prev[tableName] || [])];
427	                if (tableData[rowIndex]) {
428	                    tableData[rowIndex] = { ...tableData[rowIndex], [fieldname]: value };
429	
430	                    // Auto-calculate total for items table
431	                    if (tableName === "igf_items" && ["quantity", "estimated_rate"].includes(fieldname)) {
432	                        const qty = parseFloat(tableData[rowIndex].quantity || 0);
433	                        const rate = parseFloat(tableData[rowIndex].estimated_rate || 0);
434	                        tableData[rowIndex].estimated_cost = qty * rate;
435	                    }
436	                }
437	
438	                const next = { ...prev, [tableName]: tableData };
439	
440	                // Recalculate total estimate
441	                if (tableName === "igf_items") {
442	                    const total = tableData.reduce(
443	                        (sum: number, row: any) => sum + (parseFloat(row.estimated_cost) || 0),
444	                        0,
445	                    );
446	                    next.igf_total_estimate = total;
447	                }
448	
449	                return next;
450	            });
451	        },
452	        [],
453	    );
454	
455	    const handleTableFileChange = useCallback(
456	        (tableName: string, rowIndex: number, fieldname: string, file: File | null) => {
457	            setFormData((prev) => {
458	                const tableData = [...(prev[tableName] || [])];
459	                if (tableData[rowIndex]) {
460	                    tableData[rowIndex] = { ...tableData[rowIndex], [fieldname]: file };
461	                }
462	                return { ...prev, [tableName]: tableData };
463	            });
464	        },
465	        [],
466	    );
467	
468	    const handleAddTableRow = useCallback(
469	        (tableName: string, newRow: Record<string, any>) => {
470	            setFormData((prev) => ({
471	                ...prev,
472	                [tableName]: [...(prev[tableName] || []), newRow],
473	            }));
474	        },
475	        [],
476	    );
477	
478	    const handleDeleteTableRow = useCallback(
479	        (tableName: string, rowIndex: number) => {
480	            setFormData((prev) => {
481	                const updated = (prev[tableName] || []).filter(
482	                    (_: any, idx: number) => idx !== rowIndex,
483	                );
484	                const next = { ...prev, [tableName]: updated };
485	
486	                // Recalculate total estimate when rows are deleted
487	                if (tableName === "igf_items") {
488	                    next.igf_total_estimate = updated.reduce(
489	                        (sum: number, row: any) => sum + (parseFloat(row.estimated_cost) || 0),
490	                        0,
491	                    );
492	                }
493	
494	                return next;
495	            });
496	        },
497	        [],
498	    );
499	
500	    // --- SAVE using generic Frappe REST API ---
501	    const handleSave = async (e?: React.FormEvent) => {
502	        if (e) e.preventDefault();
503	
504	        setIsSubmitting(true);
505	        try {
506	            const currentDocName = savedDocName || editDocName;
507	            const preparedData = await prepareFormDataForApi(formData);
508	
509	            // Clean up internal fields not needed for save
510	            const payload: Record<string, any> = {};
511	            for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(preparedData)) {
512	                if (key.startsWith("_") || key === "doctype" || key === "docstatus" || key === "modified" || key === "creation" || key === "owner" || key === "modified_by") continue;
513	                payload[key] = val;
514	            }
515	            payload.doctype = DOCTYPE;
516	
517	            let result;
518	            if (currentDocName) {
519	                // Update existing
520	                result = await frappeRequest(
521	                    `/api/resource/${encodeURIComponent(DOCTYPE)}/${encodeURIComponent(currentDocName)}`,
522	                    { method: "PUT", body: JSON.stringify(payload) },
523	                );
524	            } else {
525	                // Create new
526	                result = await frappeRequest(
527	                    `/api/resource/${encodeURIComponent(DOCTYPE)}`,
528	                    { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(payload) },
529	                );
530	            }
531	
532	            const savedDoc = result.data;
533	            if (savedDoc?.name) {
534	                alert("Draft saved successfully");
535	
536	                if (!currentDocName) {
537	                    setSavedDocName(savedDoc.name);
538	                    navigate(`/general-indent/${savedDoc.name}`, { replace: true });
539	                }
540	
541	                // Refresh data
542	                setFormData(savedDoc);
543	                setWorkflowState(savedDoc.workflow_state || "Draft");
544	                setDocStatus(savedDoc.docstatus || 0);
545	                fetchWorkflowActions(savedDoc.name);
546	            } else {
547	                alert("Failed to save draft");
548	            }
549	        } catch (error: any) {
550	            console.error("Save error:", error);
551	            let errMsg = "An error occurred while saving";
552	            if (error._server_messages) {
553	                try {
554	                    const msgs = JSON.parse(error._server_messages);
555	                    errMsg = msgs.map((m: string) => {
556	                        try { return JSON.parse(m).message; } catch { return m; }
557	                    }).join("\n");
558	                } catch { /* use default */ }
559	            }
560	            alert(errMsg);
561	        } finally {
562	            setIsSubmitting(false);
563	        }
564	    };
565	
566	    // --- SUBMIT using Frappe workflow apply ---
567	    const handleWorkflowAction = async (action: string) => {
568	        const docNameToUse = savedDocName || editDocName;
569	        if (!docNameToUse) {
570	            alert("Please save the document first.");
571	            return;
572	        }
573	
574	        setIsActionLoading(true);
575	        try {
576	            // Save first if in draft
577	            if (workflowState === "Draft" || action === "Submit") {
578	                await handleSave();
579	            }
580	
581	            // Apply workflow action
582	            const res = await frappeRequest(
583	                `/api/method/frappe.model.workflow.apply_workflow`,
584	                {
585	                    method: "POST",
586	                    body: JSON.stringify({
587	                        doc: JSON.stringify({ doctype: DOCTYPE, name: docNameToUse }),
588	                        action: action,
589	                    }),
590	                },
591	            );
592	
593	            if (res.message) {
594	                alert(`Action "${action}" completed successfully`);
595	                // Refresh
596	                const updatedDoc = await fetchExistingDoc(docNameToUse);
597	                if (updatedDoc) {
598	                    setFormData(updatedDoc);
599	                    setWorkflowState(updatedDoc.workflow_state || "Draft");
600	                    setDocStatus(updatedDoc.docstatus || 0);
601	                }
602	                fetchWorkflowActions(docNameToUse);
603	            }
604	        } catch (error: any) {
605	            console.error(`Workflow Action ${action} Error:`, error);
606	            let errMsg = `An error occurred while performing action: ${action}`;
607	            if (error._server_messages) {
608	                try {
609	                    const msgs = JSON.parse(error._server_messages);
610	                    errMsg = msgs.map((m: string) => {
611	                        try { return JSON.parse(m).message; } catch { return m; }
612	                    }).join("\n");
613	                } catch { /* use default */ }
614	            }
615	            alert(errMsg);
616	        } finally {
617	            setIsActionLoading(false);
618	        }
619	    };
620	
621	    // --- Submit document (docstatus=1) ---
622	    const handleSubmit = async () => {
623	        const docNameToUse = savedDocName || editDocName;
624	        if (!docNameToUse) {
625	            alert("Please save the document first.");
626	            return;
627	        }
628	
629	        setIsActionLoading(true);
630	        try {
631	            // Save first
632	            await handleSave();
633	
634	            // Submit via Frappe method
635	            await frappeRequest(
636	                `/api/method/frappe.client.submit`,
637	                {
638	                    method: "POST",
639	                    body: JSON.stringify({
640	                        doc: JSON.stringify({ doctype: DOCTYPE, name: docNameToUse }),
641	                    }),
642	                },
643	            );
644	
645	            alert("Document submitted successfully");
646	
647	            // Refresh
648	            const updatedDoc = await fetchExistingDoc(docNameToUse);
649	            if (updatedDoc) {
650	                setFormData(updatedDoc);
651	                setWorkflowState(updatedDoc.workflow_state || "Submitted");
652	                setDocStatus(updatedDoc.docstatus || 1);
653	            }
654	        } catch (error: any) {
655	            console.error("Submit error:", error);
656	            let errMsg = "An error occurred while submitting";
657	            if (error._server_messages) {
658	                try {
659	                    const msgs = JSON.parse(error._server_messages);
660	                    errMsg = msgs.map((m: string) => {
661	                        try { return JSON.parse(m).message; } catch { return m; }
662	                    }).join("\n");
663	                } catch { /* use default */ }
664	            }
665	            alert(errMsg);
666	        } finally {
667	            setIsActionLoading(false);
668	        }
669	    };
670	
671	    // --- RENDER ---
672	    const isReadOnly = docStatus === 1 || (workflowState !== "Draft" && workflowState !== "Pending");
673	    const isSubmitted = docStatus === 1;
674	
675	    if (isLoadingFields) {
676	        return (
677	            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh] gap-4">
678	                <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-[#D97757]" />
679	                <p className="text-zinc-500 font-medium">
680	                    Loading form configuration...
681	                </p>
682	            </div>
683	        );
684	    }
685	
686	    return (
687	        <div className="bg-[#FAFAF9] dark:bg-[#18181B] min-h-screen">
688	            <main className="max-w-8xl mx-auto p-4 md:p-8 w-full overflow-hidden">
689	                {/* Header */}
690	                <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">
691	                    <button
692	                        onClick={() => navigate(-1)}
693	                        className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 transition-colors"
694	                    >
695	                        <ArrowLeft className="w-5 h-5 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400" />
696	                    </button>
697	                    <div>
698	                        <h1 className="text-2xl font-serif font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 flex items-center gap-3">
699	                            General Indent Form
700	                            {(editDocName || savedDocName) && (
701	                                <span
702	                                    className={cn(
703	                                        "text-xs font-sans px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full border",
704	                                        workflowState === "Approved"
705	                                            ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-900/20 dark:border-emerald-800/50"
706	                                            : workflowState === "Draft"
707	                                                ? "bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-700 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-700"
708	                                                : "bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200 dark:bg-blue-900/20 dark:border-blue-800/50",
709	                                    )}
710	                                >
711	                                    {workflowState}
712	                                </span>
713	                            )}
714	                        </h1>
715	                        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-1">
716	                            {editDocName || savedDocName
717	                                ? `Application ID: ${editDocName || savedDocName}`
718	                                : "New Application"}
719	                        </p>
720	                    </div>
721	                </div>
722	
723	                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6">
724	                    {/* Form Card */}
725	                    <FrappeCard>
726	                        <div className="border-b border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 px-8 py-4 flex items-center gap-3">
727	                            <div className="w-1 h-5 bg-[#D97757] rounded-full" />
728	                            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
729	                                Purchase General Form
730	                            </h2>
731	                        </div>
732	                        <div className="p-8">
733	                            <DynamicFormRenderer
734	                                fields={fields}
735	                                formData={formData}
736	                                linkOptions={linkOptions}
737	                                onChange={handleFieldChange}
738	                                onFileChange={handleFileChange}
739	                                onTableRowChange={handleTableRowChange}
740	                                onTableFileChange={handleTableFileChange}
741	                                onAddTableRow={handleAddTableRow}
742	                                onDeleteTableRow={handleDeleteTableRow}
743	                                onFieldChangeWithSideEffects={handleFieldChangeWithSideEffects}
744	                                readOnly={isReadOnly}
745	                            />
746	                        </div>
747	                    </FrappeCard>
748	
749	                    {/* Action Bar Card */}
750	                    <FrappeCard>
751	                        <div className="p-0">
752	                            {!isSubmitted && (
753	                                <div className="bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/50 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 p-6 flex items-center justify-between">
754	                                    <div className="text-sm text-zinc-500">
755	                                        {(editDocName || savedDocName) &&

805	                                                        )}

807	                                                    </FrappeButton>

809	                                            </>
810	                                        ) : (

828	                                                    ) : null}
829	                                                    {action}
830	                                                </FrappeButton>
831	                                            ))
832	                                        )}
833	                                    </div>
834	                                </div>
835	                            )}
836	                        </div>
837	                    </FrappeCard>
